Let's implement scoring via FFT:
```
def ac1_score(a, round=False):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return inf
    L = 1 << (2*a.size -1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = (A*A).real  # since linear conv with itself? zero-pad sufficient.
    b = np.fft.irfft(B, L)[:2*n-1]
    return 2*n*b.max()/s**2
```
Is using A*A correct for real linear convolution of a with itself? Yes, DFT of zero-padded a times itself = DFT of linear convolution (not autocorrelation). Since a real, B may have small imaginary due to rounding; take real. Need ensure length L >= 2n-1. Good.

Let's focus on numerical optimization.

Need choose internal scoring. We can compute exact AC1 via FFT: `score = 2*n*max(conv)/sum^2`. For a normalized sum1, score=2*n*max(conv). To avoid overflow in FFT: a values ~1/√n ~0.01, conv ~1/n ~0.0002, fine. Use real FFT.

Map ac1Score(a):
```
def score_fft(a):
 n=a.size; s=a.sum(); if s<... inf
 L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
 A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
 B=(A*A).real
 b=np.fft.irfft(B,L)[:2*n-1]
 # Due to numerical errors, b may have tiny negative; max with maybe positive
 return float(2*n*max(b.max(),0)/s/s)
```
For speed, we can cache L. Use `np.fft.rfftn`? 1D.

- Compute gradient g wrt a:
  p_i = sum_{k=i}^{i+n-1} w_k (cumulative w window length n). h_i = convolution cross: h_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}. We can compute h via FFT of a and reversed w. w length 2n-1. Let wr = w[::-1]. Lh >= n + (2n-1) -1 = 3n-2. Next power maybe 32768 for n=4806 (3n-2=14416, next 16384? 2^14=16384 yes). Good.   p via cumulative: wcum = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(w)]); p = wcum[i+n] - wcum[i] for i=0..n-1 (since w length 2n-1, i+n <=2n-1). 
  g = p*a + h.

def grad_weighted(a, w, Lc, Lh):
    n=len(a)
    wcum=np.cumsum(w, dtype=float); wcum[:]=... use np.empty
    p = (np.concatenate((wcum, [0])))? Need efficient.
    wcum_full = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    p = wcum_full[n:n+n] - wcum_full[0:n]  # length n, p_i=sum w[i:i+n]
    # h via FFT
    wr = w[::-1]
    Lh = 1 << (n + len(w)-1).bit_length()
    F_a = np.fft.rfft(a, Lh)
    F_wr=np.fft.rfft(wr,Lh)
    H=np.fft.irfft(F_a*F_wr, Lh)
    m_start = 2*n-2
    h = H[m_start-n+1:m_start+1][::-1]? Let's derive.
We need h_i = H[2n-2 - i] for i=0..n-1. So indices from 2n-2 down to n-1? For i=0 idx=2n-2, i=n-1 idx=n-1. So h = H[n-1:2n-1][::-1].
```

Let's detail C recurrence:
`r[0]=1.0; for i in 1..n-1: r[i]=r[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)`. This yields positive. Sum. Add a tiny `eps` to avoid logs zeros.

Tilt family:
```
def make_tilt(r, mu, beta=0):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n) # O(n) okay
    a=r*np.exp(mu*x)
    if beta: a*= (1+beta*x)
    a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return None
    return a/s
```
For μ large, exp(μ) if μ up to 10 okay; if 100 overflows. Use limited.

Grid μ maybe [ -2,-1,-0.5,0,0.2,0.5,0.8,1,1.3,1.6,2,2.5,3,4,5,6,8,10]. For negative, head tilt maybe worsens but include.

Could also reverse base and tilt? But root reversed is increasing; tilting head (positive μ on i/n for reversed) corresponds to decreasing root with negative? We can create rev_r and tilts, but by symmetry, score of rev_r with tilt μ equals score of r with tilt -μ? Because reversing a reverses convolution, score invariant. So scanning μ negative on r covers reversed tilt positive? If a=rev(r)*exp(μ i), reverse is r*exp(-μ (n-1-i)) ~ r*exp(μ i) times constant; score same. So one-sided scan enough.

What about adding a constant floor to r to reduce head singular and lift tail? a_i=(1-t) r_i + t * u_i where u_i maybe increasing inverse? Local perturbations:
Function `score_fft` with cached L.
```
def perturb(a, rng):
  n=len(a)
  # choose two blocks: head and tail
  style = rng.integers(0,4)
  if style==0:
     m=rng.integers(n//4, n//2)
     f=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3))
     a2=a.copy(); a2[:m]*=1/f; a2[-m:]*=f  # shift mass
  elif style==1:
     block size w ...
     f=exp(normal)
     a2[start:stop]*=f; renorm
  else:
     random log perturbation with smooth low-freq? 
  a2=np.maximum(a2,0); s=a2.sum(); if s>0 a2/=s
```
Accept if score < best - 1e-12. Use time to do e.g. 2000-10000 steps. Since starting near optimum, small perturbations. Need avoid too large steps; adaptive σ. Start σ=0.2, reduce if acceptance low, increase if high. Specifically maintain `step = 0.1`. At each trial, add noise amplitude step to selected block log factor. If accepted, step*=1.1; else step*=0.97. Also occasionally mix block sizes.

But random local search may be slow. Gradient L2 might be better.

Let's implement both: after grid initial best, run gradient descent on L2 (smooth) for a fixed number, then local random, then gradient max, then local again. Use exact score for best updates.

Gradient L2 in direct a with projected gradient and line search may be easier than log Adam. Let's try to design stable direct optimizer for J:
- `J = sum(b*b)`. Since sum fixed, if J decreases, likely score. We can do a few iterations.
- Compute b, g = 2*grad_weighted(a, b). Project: g -= mean(g) (to keep sum approx zero). Normalize g RMS=1.

Implement log Adam for L2:
```
def optimize_log(a0, mode, iters, lr=0.5):
 u=np.log(a0+1e-15); u-=u.mean()
 m=v=zeros
 best_a=a0.copy(); best_score=score(a0); best_J=...
 stall=0
 for it:
   a=softmax(u)
   b, sc, J = compute
   if sc<best_score: update
   if mode=='var': w=b
   else: w=smooth_weights(b, gamma)
   g=grad_weighted(a,w,Lh)
   grad_u=a*(g - dot(a,g))
   # normalize grad_u by its L2 norm plus maybe by 1 to use lr
   norm=np.linalg.norm(grad_u)/np.sqrt(n)
   if norm>0: grad_u/=norm
   m=0.9*m+0.1*grad_u; v=0.999*v+0.001*grad_u*grad_u
   mhat=m/(1-0.9**it+eps); vhat=...
   u -= lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-6)
   u -= u.mean()
   u = np.clip(u, -50, 50)
   # line search? periodic: after update, compute new; if score worsens too much for 10, lr*=0.5
```
`lr` with normalized gradient RMS=1. In log space, u values ~log a. Typical a small, log ~ -8 to -2. Step 0.1 changes relative 10%. Use lr=0.2 maybe. Adam adapts. We can set lr=0.05 for safety. If score improves, can maintain.

```
b_target = flat (1/L)

  if any negative (due to numerical) clip.
  a = normalize
  b = conv(a,a)
  b_target = b_target + ρ (flat - b)  # flow to flat
  # or b_target = max(flat, b?)
  speed
```

Define `sqrt_left(target_left)`:
```
a=np.zeros(n)
a[0]=sqrt(max(target_left[0],0))
for k in 1..n-1:
    s=0
    # sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i*a_{k-i}

Local search function details to be efficient:
```
def local_search(a0, max_steps, deadline, rng, step=0.05):
   n=len(a0); scorer=...
   a=a0.copy(); best=a0.copy(); best_sc=scorer.score(a)
   x=... maybe precompute
   cur_sc=best_sc
   for step_i in range(max_steps):
      if time> deadline: break
      a2=a.copy()
      kind=rng.integers(5)
      if kind==0: head/tail shift
         h= int(rng.integers(max(1,n//50), n//3+1))
         t= int(...)
         d = rng.normal(0, step)
         a2[:h] *= np.exp(-d)
         a2[n-t:] *= np.exp(d)
      elif kind==1: block
         w= int(rng.integers(max(1,n//50), n//5+1))
         s= int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
         a2[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,step))
      elif kind==2: lowfreq random multiplicative
         freqs = rng.uniform(0.1, 3, size=5)
         ph=rng.uniform(0,2π,5)
         f = np.sum(np.exp( (step/np.sqrt(5))*rng.normal(loc=0) * np.sin(2π*freqs*x + ph) for ...))
         a2 *= np.exp(f)
      elif kind==3: tilt
         mu = rng.normal(0, step)
         a2 *= np.exp(mu*x)
      elif kind==4: global noise log
         u=np.log(a+1e-300) + rng.normal(0, step/np.sqrt(n), n)
         a2=softmax(u)
      a2 = np.clip(a2,0,None); ssum=a2.sum(); if ssum>0: a2/=ssum
      sc=scorer.score(a2)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best=a2.copy()
          if sc < cur_sc: a=a2.copy(); cur_sc=sc
          # maybe reduce step slightly
      elif sc < cur_sc + step*0.05: # allow small worse to walk
          a=a2.copy(); cur_sc=sc
      # adapt step
      if accepted best or cur: step = min(0.5, step*1.05)
      else: step = max(0.005, step*0.97)
      # random reset occasionally if stuck
      if i%2000==0 and time left: a=best.copy() # keep near best
   return best,best_sc
```

Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def make_scorer(n):
  L=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Lc=2*n-1; Lh=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
  def conv(a):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:Lc].real
  def score(a):
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.inf
    b=conv(a)
    mx=b.max()
    if mx<0: mx=0
    return float(2*n*mx/(s*s))
  def grad(a,w):
    wcum=np.empty(len(w)+1); wcum[0]=0; np.cumsum(w,out=wcum[1:])
    p=wcum[n:n+n]-wcum[:n]
    wr=w[::-1]
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Lh); Fwr=np.fft.rfft(wr,Lh)
    H=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fwr,Lh)
    h=H[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return h + p*a
  return score,conv,grad
```
Potential memory/time: In `score`, for local search every call computes conv. `grad` computes another conv via FFT. OK.

`softmax`:
```
def _softmax(u):
 u=np.asarray(u,float)
 u=u-u.max()
 e=np.exp(u)
 s=e.sum()
 if not finite or s==0: return np.ones(n)/n
 return e/s
```

`central_binomial(n)`:
```
r=np.empty(n); r[0]=1.
for i in range(1,n): r[i]=r[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
return r
```
O(n) Python loop 4096 okay. Could vectorize cumulative product but loop fine.

Candidate normalize:
```
def norm_a(a): a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a=a/s; else return None; return a
```
For finite: check np.isfinite.

 for q in (...): add(r**q)

    else: factor=1+b*x*(1-x); add(r*factor)
 # uniform

 # random starts

 # parent if length n? If parent available and len==n, add normalized; if len different, maybe downsample via np.resize? Should avoid altering? Could interpolate but not necessary.

Adam function:
```
def adam_search(a0, scorer, conv, grad, steps, deadline, mode='var', gamma=20, lr=0.05):
  n=len(a0)
  u=np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300)); u-=u.mean()
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); beta1=.9; beta2=.999; eps=1e-8
  best=a0.copy(); best_sc=scorer(a0); cur_sc=best_sc; lr_cur=lr; bad=0
  for it in range(steps):
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
    a=_softmax(u)
    b=conv(a)
    sc=float(2*n*b.max()/(a.sum()**2)) # sum 1
    if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); bad=0
    else:
      if sc > best_sc*1.05: bad +=1
      else: bad=0
    # Maybe if bad too many, reset and reduce
    if bad > 30:
       u=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); u-=u.mean(); m=v=0; lr_cur*=0.7; bad=0
    if mode=='var':
      w=b; w=np.maximum(w,0)
      if w.sum()==0: break
      g=grad(a,w)
    else:
      cmax=b.max()
      if cmax<=0: break
      z=(b/cmax)**gamma
      zsum=z.sum()
      if zsum<=0: w=np.zeros_like(b); w[-1]=1
      else: w=z/zsum
      g=grad(a,w)
    dot=np.dot(a,g)
    gu=a*(g-dot)
    # if norm tiny break
    norm=np.linalg.norm(gu)
    if not finite or norm==0: break
    m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*gu
    v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*gu*gu
    hat1=m/(1-beta1**(it+1))
    hat2=v/(1-beta2**(it+1))
    u -= lr_cur * hat1/(np.sqrt(hat2)+eps)
    u -= u.mean()
    u=np.clip(u, -40, 40)
  return best,best_sc
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    a /= s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    return a


def _softmax(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    u = u - u.max()
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        n = u.size
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _central_binomial(n):
    r = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    r[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        r[i] = r[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
    return r


def _make_scorer(n):
    n = int(n)
    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(Lc)
    Lh = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    def conv(a):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        return np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:Lc].real

    def score(a):
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return float("inf")
        b = conv(a)
        mx = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
        if mx < 0.0:
            mx = 0.0
        return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))

    def grad(a, w):
        if w.size != Lc:
            raise ValueError("bad gradient weight size")
        wc = np.empty(Lc + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        wc[0] = 0.0
        np.cumsum(w, out=wc[1:])
        p = wc[n:n + n] - wc[:n]

        wr = w[::-1]
        Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Lh)
        Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, Lh)
        H = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fw, Lh)
        h = H[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        return h + p * a

    return score, conv, grad


def _adam_search(a0, scorer, conv, grad, deadline, mode="var", lr=0.06, gamma=12.0):
    n = a0.size
    u = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    u -= u.mean()

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1, b2, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8

    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = scorer(a0)
    cur_sc = best_sc
    bad = 0
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a = _softmax(u)
        b = conv(a)
        s = float(a.sum())
        sc = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(b.max())) / (s * s)) if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0 else float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            bad = 0
        cur_sc = sc if np.isfinite(sc) else best_sc
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc > best_sc * 1.08:
            bad += 1
        else:
            bad = 0

        if bad > 35:
            u = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
            u -= u.mean()
            m[:] = 0.0
            v[:] = 0.0
            lr_cur = max(0.005, 0.72 * lr_cur)
            bad = 0

        if mode == "var":
            w = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                break
            g = grad(a, w / ws)
        else:
            cmax = float(b.max())
            if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
                break
            z = (b / cmax)
            z[z < 0.0] = 0.0
            z = np.power(z, float(gamma))
            zsum = float(z.sum())
            if zsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
                break
            w = z / zsum
            g = grad(a, w)

        dot = float(np.dot(a, g))
        gu = a * (g - dot)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(gu))
        if not np.isfinite(norm) or norm <= 0.0:
            break

        it += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gu
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gu * gu)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** it)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** it)
        u -= lr_cur * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        u -= u.mean()
        u = np.clip(u, -40.0, 40.0)

    return best_a, best_sc


def _local_search(a0, scorer, deadline, rng, step=0.09):
    n = a0.size
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = scorer(a)
    cur_sc = best_sc
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    cur_step = float(step)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a2 = a.copy()
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 5))

        if kind == 0:
            h = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 60), max(2, n // 4)))
            t = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 60), max(2, n // 4)))
            d = float(rng.normal(0.0, cur_step))
            a2[:h] *= np.exp(-d)
            a2[n - t:] *= np.exp(d)
        elif kind == 1:
            w = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 120), max(2, n // 6)))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            a2[st:st + w] *= np.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, cur_step)))
        elif kind == 2:
            mu = float(rng.normal(0.0, cur_step))
            a2 *= np.exp(mu * x)
        elif kind == 3:
            mid = n // 2
            d = float(rng.normal(0.0, cur_step))
            a2[:mid] *= np.exp(-d)
            a2[mid:] *= np.exp(d)
        else:
            f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for _ in range(5):
                f += float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.sin(
                    2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.uniform(0.1, 4.0)) * x + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                )
            f *= cur_step / np.sqrt(5.0)
            a2 *= np.exp(f)

        a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
        ss = float(a2.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            continue
        a2 /= ss
        sc = scorer(a2)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            a = a2.copy()
            cur_sc = sc
            cur_step = min(0.45, cur_step * 1.06)
        elif sc < cur_sc + 0.02 * cur_step:
            a = a2.copy()
            cur_sc = sc
            cur_step = min(0.45, cur_step * 1.03)
        else:
            cur_step = max(0.012, cur_step * 0.975)

        it += 1
        if it % 1200 == 0:
            if not np.isfinite(cur_sc) or cur_sc > best_sc * 1.04:
                a = best_a.copy()
                cur_sc = best_sc

    return best_a, best_sc


def _generate_candidates(n, scorer, rng):
    r = _central_binomial(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    rev = r[::-1]
    cands = []

    def add(a):
        a = _normalize(a)
        if a is None:
            return
        sc = scorer(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append((float(sc), a))

    for q in (0.75, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, 1.3):
        add(np.power(r, q))

    for mu in (-1.0, -0.5, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        add(r * np.exp(mu * x))

    for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.6):
        add(r + t * rev)

    for b in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, -0.1, -0.3, -0.5, -0.7):
        if b < 0:
            f = 1.0 + b * x
            if f.min() > 0:
                add(r * f)
        else:
            add(r * (1.0 + b * x * (1.0 - x)))
            add(r * (1.0 + b * np.power(x, 2.0)))

    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for _ in range(6):
        u = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        u = np.convolve(u, np.ones(max(3, n // 200)) / max(3.0, n // 200), mode="same")
        add(_softmax(u))

    cands.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])
    return [a for _, a in cands[:12]]


def _search_length(n, deadline, rng, store, best_state):
    if n < 16:
        return
    if deadline - time.monotonic() <= 2.0:
        return

    scorer, conv, grad = _make_scorer(n)
    cands = []
    try:
        cands = _generate_candidates(n, scorer, rng)
    except Exception:
        cands = []

    if not cands:
        try:
            base = _central_binomial(n)
            cands = [_normalize(base)]
        except Exception:
            cands = [_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))]

    def consider(a, sc):
        a = _normalize(a)
        if a is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        if sc < best_state[0]:
            best_state[0] = float(sc)
            best_state[1] = a.copy()
        if len(store) < 80 or sc < best_state[0] + 0.08:
            store.append((float(sc), a.copy()))
            if len(store) > 120:
                store.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])
                del store[120:]

    active_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, 0.55 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
    top = cands
    if active_deadline - time.monotonic() <= 1.0:
        top = top[:3]

    for a in top:
        sc = scorer(a)
        consider(a, sc)

    if time.monotonic() >= active_deadline:
        return

    # Local refinement for the strongest candidates.
    top = sorted([(scorer(a), a) for a in top], key=lambda t: t[0])[:6]
    rem = max(1.0, active_deadline - time.monotonic())
    for idx, (sc0, a0) in enumerate(top):
        sub_deadline = min(active_deadline, time.monotonic() + rem / float(len(top) - idx))
        if time.monotonic() >= sub_deadline:
            break
        try:
            a1, sc1 = _local_search(a0, scorer, sub_deadline, rng, step=0.085)
            consider(a1, sc1)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if active_deadline - time.monotonic() <= 1.0:
        return

    cand = best_state[1] if best_state[1] is not None and len(best_state[1]) == n else (top[0][1] if top else _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
    if cand is None:
        return

    rem = max(1.0, active_deadline - time.monotonic())
    for mode, lr, gm, frac in (("var", 0.055, 10.0, 0.45), ("max", 0.045, 10.0, 0.55)):
        sub_deadline = min(active_deadline, time.monotonic() + frac * rem)
        if mode == "max":
            sub_deadline = active_deadline
        if time.monotonic() >= sub_deadline:
            break
        try:
            a1, sc1 = _adam_search(cand, scorer, conv, grad, sub_deadline, mode=mode, lr=lr, gamma=gm)
            consider(a1, sc1)
            cand = a1
        except Exception:
            pass

    # One final small local polish.
    if active_deadline - time.monotonic() > 3.0:
        try:
            a1, sc1 = _local_search(cand, scorer, active_deadline, rng, step=0.06)
            consider(a1, sc1)
        except Exception:
            pass


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget_s - 35.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    store = []
    best_state = [float("inf"), None]

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            parent = _normalize(np.maximum(p, 0.0))
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    rhs = best_state[0]  # placeholder to satisfy name usage

    # Phase 1: short length for a quick valid candidate.
    if budget_s > 120:
        d1 = t0 + max(25.0, 0.14 * (hard_deadline - t0))
        _search_length(1024, d1, rng, store, best_state)

    # Phase 2: medium length, main optimization.
    if budget_s > 260:
        d2 = t0 + max(80.0, 0.58 * (hard_deadline - t0))
        _search_length(2048, min(d2, hard_deadline), rng, store, best_state)

    # Phase 3: longer length if time remains.
    if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 60.0:
        d3 = min(hard_deadline, t0 + max(120.0, 0.92 * (hard_deadline - t0)))
        _search_length(4096, d3, rng, store, best_state)

    # Include the provided parent, if available, as a trustworthy fallback.
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            # Score parent with the authoritative evaluator only if there is time.
            if time.monotonic() + 5.0 < hard_deadline:
                sc = float(evaluate_sequence(parent.tolist()))
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc < best_state[0]:
                        best_state[0] = sc
                        best_state[1] = parent.copy()
                    if len(store) < 40:
                        store.append((sc, parent.copy()))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Choose final candidate by exact evaluation among the best FFT-scored candidates.
    final_candidates = []
    if best_state[1] is not None:
        final_candidates.append((best_state[0], best_state[1].copy()))
    if parent is not None and not any((a is parent or len(a) == len(parent)) for _, a in final_candidates):
        final_candidates.append((float("inf"), parent.copy()))

    if store:
        store.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])
        seen = set()
        for sc, a in store:
            key = (len(a), float(np.dot(a, a)) if a.size < 20000 else None)
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            final_candidates.append((sc, a.copy()))
            if len(final_candidates) >= 16:
                break

    final_candidates.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])

    exact_best_score = float("inf")
    exact_best_list = None

    for sc, a in final_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() + 6.0 >= hard_deadline and exact_best_list is not None:
            break
        try:
            pa = _normalize(a)
            if pa is None:
                continue
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(pa.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = val
                exact_best_list = pa.tolist()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if exact_best_list is not None:
        return exact_best_list

    # If exact evaluation did not complete, return the best finite fallback.
    if best_state[1] is not None:
        a = _normalize(best_state[1])
        if a is not None:
            return [float(x) for x in a]
    if parent is not None:
        return [float(x) for x in parent]

    # Last-resort valid sequence.
    a = _normalize(np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64))
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```
